# iOS visual parity — C++ MAUI port vs real .NET MAUI

Single source of truth for iOS pixel parity. Each example is rendered by BOTH the C++ port and real .NET MAUI on the **same** simulator, then compared deterministically (pixel diff + SSIM) and categorized by two independent vision models.

The Demo column now carries a **third sub-column, C++&XAML** — the gallery page rendered from MAUI XAML markup loaded at runtime (vs the hand-written C++ builder). Its faithfulness assessment (50 twins: **41 match / 9 minor / 0 major** vs the builder — **100% match-or-minor, zero majors** — and **38 / 11 / 1** vs MAUI (98%), established by a consistent full re-judge + a 3-independent-judge majority-vote denoise, after the W7–W17 loader features (gradient-brush, FormattedString/Span, Border.StrokeShape, AbsoluteLayout, FlexLayout, Picker.Items, x:Array→ItemsSource with the `{Binding .}` render path, ItemsLayout grid, Button.ImageSource, ControlTemplate/ContentPresenter, FontImageSource) and re-authoring of the simplified twins; the binding/ItemsSource cluster, relative_layout, radio_template_from_style (real ControlTemplate), and image-vs-builder (FontImageSource glyphs) are all closed, leaving only **1 vs-MAUI major** — `image`, structural (maui-compare renders FileSource blank where the port renders it, a harness artifact per parity-policy rule 3), not a loader/authoring gap) lives in **[XAML_PARITY.md](XAML_PARITY.md)**.

## Setup

- **Simulator:** iPhone 17 (1206x2622 native, no Display Zoom) · UDID `C4926671-2FA7-428E-B4A4-480692EE742B` · both apps on the same instance, no Display Zoom.
- **C++ port:** `dev.maui-cpp.ios-gallery` (current branch build).
- **.NET MAUI baseline:** `com.companyname.mauicompare` on **MAUI 10.0.71** (stable; matches git tag `10.0.71` used as the C# oracle). The app declares `UILaunchScreen` so it renders native (a missing one previously letterboxed it ~1.25×).
- **Deterministic diff:** Pillow per-pixel delta + ImageMagick DSSIM→SSIM, status-bar clock masked. "Pixel-perfect" = SSIM ≥ 0.99 both themes (byte-exact 0 is unreachable cross-engine due to anti-aliasing).
- **Vision models:** Claude Sonnet `claude-sonnet-4-6` and Gemini `gemini-2.5-flash` (exact id per row).
- **Animated pages** (🎬) carry GIFs (+ mp4 archive in `captures/`); interactive-only pages show the idle frame.
- **Generated:** 2026-06-24. Diff data: `diff_results.json`; per-model: `analysis_*`.

## Summary

172 examples · pixel-perfect-grade **12** + match **4**. The two vision models judge independently and diverge often (Gemini runs stricter), so the **consensus** below is conservative: a page is *major* only when BOTH models flag it, *pixel-perfect/match* only when both agree (+ SSIM), and the uncertain middle falls to *minor*. The per-page Sonnet & Gemini columns carry the raw, independent verdicts.

**Consensus classification:**

| Classification | Count | Examples |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 🟢 Pixel-perfect | 12 | activity_indicator, grid, relative_layout, alignment, polyline_gallery, shape_app_theme, empty_view_load_simulate, swipe_item_position, pan_gesture_events, menu_bar, chrome, custom_layout |
| 🟢 Match | 4 | single_bound_selection, custom_size_swipe, ios_pan_gesture, modal |
| 🟡 C++ minor | 126 | label, entry, editor, search_bar, picker, date_picker, pickers, stepper, check_box, progress_bar, indicator, image, box_view, content_view, containers, controls_stack, input_controls, fonts, formatted_text, styles, triggers, behaviors, semantics, app_theme_binding … (+102) |
| 🔴 C++ major | 29 | button, time_picker, slider, switch, image_button, layout_is_enabled, path_transform_string, border_playground, border_clip_playground, clip, clip_views, clip_gallery, shadow_playground, invalidate_shadow_host, collectionview, preselected_items, header_footer_grid, header_footer_grid_horizontal, header_footer_template, header_footer_view, nested_collection, cv_visual_states, items_updating_scroll_mode, radio_button_content … (+5) |
| ◻️ MAUI blank | 1 | radio_template_from_style |

**Per-model distribution** (independent; family agreement ≈ 43%):

| Category | Sonnet `claude-sonnet-4-6` | Gemini `gemini-2.5-flash` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 🟢 Pixel-perfect | 0 | 2 |
| 🟢 Match | 53 | 21 |
| 🟡 C++ minor | 86 | 59 |
| 🔴 C++ major | 30 | 90 |
| 🟠 MAUI minor | 1 | 0 |
| 🟣 MAUI major | 1 | 0 |
| ◻️ MAUI blank | 1 | 0 |

note in red/italic, and four compact card entries (Slavko Vlasic, Carolina Pena, Wade Blanks, Colette Quint).<br>• MAUI renders the CardView items with a gray image placeholder square (left) and multi-line text (right); C++ renders the same structure with visible placeholder squares and text.<br>• C++ light cards appear slightly wider/more stretched horizontally; MAUI insets the content more due to the harness wrapper.<br>• Dark mode both sides match the card structure; slight spacing differences between card rows are minor. | **🔴 C++ major**<br><sub>gemini-3.1-flash-lite</sub><br>• The image boxes in the C++ implementation are significantly larger than the MAUI reference.<br>• The text layout and line wrapping are inconsistent between the two implementations.<br>• The overall vertical spacing and padding between elements do not match the MAUI reference.<br>• The font weight and sizing appear slightly different, affecting the overall visual density. |
| 171 | **Custom Layout** | <table><tr><td align="center">MAUI light<br><img src="captures/maui_light/custom_layout.png" height="350"></td><td align="center">C++ light<br><img src="captures/cpp_light/custom_layout.png" height="350"></td><td align="center">C++&amp;XAML light<br><img src="captures/xaml_light/custom_layout.png" height="350"></td></tr><tr><td align="center">MAUI dark<br><img src="captures/maui_dark/custom_layout.png" height="350"></td><td align="center">C++ dark<br><img src="captures/cpp_dark/custom_layout.png" height="350"></td><td align="center">C++&amp;XAML dark<br><img src="captures/xaml_dark/custom_layout.png" height="350"></td></tr></table> | ports CustomLayoutPage.xaml (+ CustomLayoutPage.xaml.cs) | **🟢 Match**<br><sub>claude-sonnet-4-6</sub><br>• Both show Top label at top, Bottom label at bottom, Left/Left on left, Right/Right on right<br>• C++ Left/Right labels appear horizontally shifted outward vs MAUI (wider spread)<br>• Overall structure and custom layout logic matches — same element types and arrangement<br>• Dark mode reproduces the same minor spacing difference | **🟢 Match**<br><sub>gemini-3.1-flash-lite</sub><br>• The layout and positioning of all labels (Top, Left, Right, Bottom) are visually identical between the MAUI and C++ implementations.<br>• The color schemes for both light and dark modes are correctly applied and match the reference.<br>• The minor pixel differences reported are negligible and likely due to sub-pixel rendering or anti-aliasing variations. |
| 172 | **Visual States** | <table><tr><td align="center">MAUI light<br><img src="captures/maui_light/visual_states.png" height="350"></td><td align="center">C++ light<br><img src="captures/cpp_light/visual_states.png" height="350"></td></tr><tr><td align="center">MAUI dark<br><img src="captures/maui_dark/visual_states.png" height="350"></td><td align="center">C++ dark<br><img src="captures/cpp_dark/visual_states.png" height="350"></td></tr></table> | ports VisualStatesPage.xaml | **🟢 Match**<br><sub>claude-sonnet-4-6</sub><br>• Both sides show the same content: Entry with VisualStateManager (green bar), Entry to enable 2nd Entry, Button with Normal/PointerOver visual states with explanatory text, and a blue link &#x27;Click me to see the state change and revert&#x27;<br>• The green bar above the first Entry, placeholder text, and explanatory label text all match between MAUI and C++<br>• The 8.6% pixel diff / 0.94 SSIM is likely driven by font rendering differences in the multi-line label paragraphs (text flow/wrapping may differ slightly) rather than structural layout differences<br>• Dark mode matches light mode behavior equivalently on both sides | **🔴 C++ major**<br><sub>gemini-3.1-flash-lite</sub><br>• The C++ implementation has significantly different font weights and text rendering compared to the MAUI reference.<br>• The text alignment and line spacing are inconsistent between the two implementations.<br>• The input field borders and background colors do not match the MAUI reference.<br>• The overall layout spacing and padding are noticeably different, leading to a different visual structure. |

</details>

<!-- MACOS:BEGIN -->

<details>
<summary><h2>macOS (172 examples) — click to expand</h2></summary>

.NET MAUI on macOS *is* **Mac Catalyst** (UIKit), so the Catalyst row of each cell is the strict 3-way parity board — **MAUI ┃ C++ ┃ C++&amp;XAML** — rendering the same gallery the iOS section does, captured by `tools/parity/capture_maccatalyst.py`. **AppKit** is the native-NSView backend (**C++ ┃ C++&amp;XAML**), captured by `tools/parity/capture_appkit.py`; being a different UI framework it targets *completeness* (every coded/XAML element present) and **C++ == C++&amp;XAML**, not pixel parity. In each screenshot cell the **AppKit C++ / C++&amp;XAML** shots sit directly under the matching **Catalyst C++ / C++&amp;XAML** shots (the first AppKit column is intentionally empty — AppKit has no MAUI reference). Every shot is shown light over dark. See [maccatalyst/APPKIT_FINDINGS.md](maccatalyst/APPKIT_FINDINGS.md) and [../MACOS_ANDROID_RESUME.md](../MACOS_ANDROID_RESUME.md).

**Coverage:** 50 / 172 Catalyst C++ boards · 50 / 172 AppKit C++ boards (light + dark each).

**Classification:** the C++-vs-MAUI visual review has **not run yet** for macOS, so every example is currently *pending*. The table below keeps the iOS consensus shape so it can be filled in the same way once the review runs.

| --- | --- | --- |
| 🟢 Pixel-perfect | 0 | — |
| 🟢 Match | 0 | — |
| 🟡 C++ minor | 0 | — |
| 🔴 C++ major | 0 | — |
| ⏳ Pending review | 172 | Catalyst + AppKit boards captured where handlers exist; vision review outstanding |

<details>
<summary><h2>Android (172 examples) — click to expand</h2></summary>

The C++ port's gallery pages rendered by the **Android app host** (a real Activity/APK built by `tools/parity/build_android_apphost.sh` — aapt2/d8/apksigner, no gradle) on the `maui-test` emulator, captured via `adb screencap`. Per the cross-platform goal, every iOS example has an Android render here. Pages built on controls whose **Android handler is not yet implemented** (CollectionView, Picker, Date/TimePicker, Border/Shapes, …) render blank or partial — the honest current state of the Android backend (the layout/container handlers + ~12 widget handlers are done; see [../MACOS_ANDROID_RESUME.md](../MACOS_ANDROID_RESUME.md)). The **MAUI** and **C++&amp;XAML** Android columns are future work (need MauiCompare-android + the gallery_xaml app host); until those land every cell in them is a `pending` placeholder so the row footprint matches the eventual 3-up board.

**Coverage:** 172 / 172 pages captured (C++ column); MAUI 0 / 172, C++&amp;XAML 0 / 172.

**Classification:** the C++-vs-MAUI visual review has **not run yet** for Android (it needs the MAUI Android column as the oracle), so every example is currently *pending*. The table below keeps the iOS consensus shape so it can be filled in the same way once the MAUI captures exist.
